- «རུས་མིག» (q219): the reprint's ra stands with the Zhol etext's; the Zhol scan does not settle it by eye (claim [ch27-rus-mig-bone-eye](../../claims/ch27-rus-mig-bone-eye.md)).
- «རྡེལ་ཁྲ» (q215, vol. 1 p. 468, l. 23): read as «རྡེལ་ཁྲ» on the reprint; its rite is the 26th's speckled rite and the board it names the mixed one (claim [wb1-p468-rdel-khra-whole-board](../../claims/wb1-p468-rdel-khra-whole-board.md)).
- KD p. 726's household round for seven members: not settled at 1002 px (claim [kd3-p726-household-round-not-read](../../claims/kd3-p726-household-round-not-read.md)).

- The la's wandering (q363): the Taikhang carves the snake's and horse's la north and the pig's south, the Zhol print the same by Yigdzin-1; the rat's «བླ་ལྷོར» is read ལྟོར by Yigdzin-1 on the Taikhang and not settled by eye (claim [ch27-taikhang-la-wandering](../../claims/ch27-taikhang-la-wandering.md), passage [wb.2:p16b](#wb-2-p16b)).
- The turtle's wood verse (q364): «ལྷག་མ་བཅུ་པོ», ten, on the Taikhang and the Zhol print, read by agy and Yigdzin-1, and «ཤིང་ལུག་པ» with པ ([ch27-taikhang-turtle-ten](../../claims/ch27-taikhang-turtle-ten.md), [wb.2:p20b](#wb-2-p20b)).
- The doubtful letters (q366): p. 9, l. 6 «མག་རྒུད» on the Taikhang against «མག་སྐུད» in the 1996 print and on the Zhol print by Yigdzin-1; p. 17, l. 9 «རྔམས» by three readers, not told from «ཛ[?]» by eye; p. 17, l. 26 a box-shaped first letter, བ or འ, not ཞ ([ch27-taikhang-doubtful-letters](../../claims/ch27-taikhang-doubtful-letters.md), [wb.2:p9b](#wb-2-p9b), [wb.2:p17b](#wb-2-p17b), [wb.2:p17c](#wb-2-p17c)).
- The Taikhang and the Zhol print are not one impression: where they differ (p. 9 «རྒུད» against «སྐུད», p. 17 l. 26 «བུར» against «འུར», the same line's «ཆུ་མིག» against «ཆུ་མག», both by Yigdzin-1) the second reading is the machine's alone until the line is read at a larger size.

## Read further 2026-10-28 (RD62)

*Read: scan, 2026-10-28, the words named below; the rest of each page as before.*

Read again on the Gser thang reprint (I3CN5029 img. 22, l. 23; img. 23, l. 9 and l. 26; img. 15, l. 6; the lines cut with `scans.py line`, enlarged 5 to 6 times) against the readings of RD51 and RD57; no model run and no agy turn, the reprint's 1-bit scan giving nothing new for a reader to choose between.

- The la's wandering (q363): the rat verse «བྱི་བའི་ལོ་པ་བླ་ལྷོར་འཁྱམས» has a final ར after a heavy stack; the head of the stack stays a blot at 5 times, so ལྷོར against ལྟོར is still decided by sense (south, as the pig's «ལྷོ་ཡི»). The pairs rat and pig south, snake and horse north stand on every print read (reprint, Zhol, Sakya etext, Taikhang): unsettled whether the tradition means them, the question stays.
- The golden turtle (q364), a check of the sum by the verse itself: in the other four elements the remainder is twelve less the years named (fire four named, «བརྒྱད» eight; iron three named, «དགུ» nine; water four named, «བརྒྱད» eight; earth four named, «བརྒྱད» eight). In wood three are named (rat, ox, sheep), so the remainder by the same rule is nine, not the printed «བཅུ»; ten would be right only if the sheep were not named, and then the cow would have no year though it has its two white pebbles. The printed ten stands on all four prints read, so it is a slip carried by the block, as the sum of sixty shows; this is our own count, not a word of the text.
- «བརྗེ» (q365): read as printed on the reprint (img. 29, l. 22); no text read spells the monk so; the set is the monk's by place and content, as in RD51; unsettled whether «བརྗེ» is a spelling of བཙུན.
- p. 17, l. 9: the syllable after «ལྕོང་མོའི» is a ཛ-shaped stack with a left notch followed by «མས» at 6 times; ཛ against ར over ང is not told by the eye (Yigdzin-1 and agy read «རྔམས»); still unsettled. p. 17, l. 26: the syllable before «ཞོགས» has a round bowl and no ཞ stem, so «འུར», as RD51 read it; against the Taikhang's བ or འ the reprint is on the side of འ.
- p. 9, l. 6: after «མག» the stack of the reprint has a filled head and ུ; at 6 times it is not told between «སྐུད» and «རྒུད»; the Taikhang's «རྒུད» (RD57) is the likelier by sense, the reprint left as printed «སྐུད». p. 9, l. 16 and the meaning of «ན་ཟླ», «ཚད་ཟླ»: nothing new.
- q216, q217, q225, q259 (the meadow's verdicts, Sprugtön's limbs, the five totals, whose element): nothing on the scan beyond RD54's and RD38's answers; they stand as narrowed there.

## Read further 2026-10-28 (RD67)

*Read: etext and search, 2026-10-28, on the prints other than the Gser thang reprint; the ZG page on its scan (I4PD2444 img. 344-345, left halves enlarged 1.6 times, the rest at page size); no model run, no agy turn.*

The etexts of the 1996 print, the Gser thang print, the Sakya print (WB2, vol. 2 etext I1KG12740, machine reading), the Zhol print (WBZ, I1KG1710, machine reading) and BDRC's exact-phrase search over every etext were searched for the words of questions 363-366 (claim [ch27-other-prints-and-etexts](../../claims/ch27-other-prints-and-etexts.md)); the poor etext of the Taikhang-copy impression (MW4CZ74207, I4CZ74285) reaches only the horse verse.

- The la's wandering (q363): rat south, snake north, horse north, pig south stands also in the Sakya and Zhol etexts and in the search's hits of the 1996 and Gser thang etexts; nothing new, the question stays open.
- The golden turtle (q364): the wood verse's «བཅུ་པོ» ten is read by the Sakya and Zhol etexts too, so on six prints; the wood sheep's «ཤིང་ལུག་པ» has པ in the Sakya etext as in the Gser thang print. Unchanged: our count of nine is ours.
- The monk's set (q365): «བརྗེའི» is also the word of ch. 30's recap of the reckonings (vol. 2, p. 155, in the 1996 etext, already in [wb.2:p152-156](ch30.md#wb-2-p152-156)) and of the rare-texts print's search snippet, so a word of WB, not of one block; its meaning is still not found; unsettled.
- The doubtful letters (q366, q407): the Sakya etext has «མག་སྐུད» at p. 9, l. 6 beside the Zhol etext's, and «འུར་ཞོགས» at p. 17, l. 26 beside the Zhol print and the Gser thang reprint; the Taikhang's «མག་རྒུད» and «བུར» stand alone against three prints, but each of the three is a machine reading. It would make the Taikhang an impression of a different block or a recut, not settled. «ལྕོང་མོའི་རྔམས» is found in no etext.
- q216, q225: Zhechen Gyaltsab's verse on the meadow's ornament leaves (zg:p300-301, [zg-meadow-five-totals](../../claims/zg-meadow-five-totals.md)): after the twenty-five pebbles of a group are fought, the five verdicts are "gathered into five general totals" by life and lifespan, body and illness, power with food and wealth (ཟ་ཐང, za thang), luck with merit, and the mewa with demons: a verdict for each aspect, which reads as the first branch of q216; the totals are the *Moonbeams*' pairs for q225, with «ཟ་ཐང» for the power as there. The notes' "five of one animal" stands beside it; the two are not shown to differ.
- q217 (Sprugtön's sixteen limbs): nothing; neither ZG nor the other etexts name the limbs.
- q259: ZG's own example (zg:p303-304, RD44) is already in [circles-zg-worked](../../claims/circles-zg-worked.md); nothing further.

## Read further 2026-10-28 (RD70)

*Read: scan, 2026-10-28: Zhechen Gyaltsab's page (zg:p300-301, I4PD2444 img. 344-345, crops of the sum lines at 2×, the rest at page size) and the *Moonbeams*' folio of the same leaves (dz:i050, I2533 img. 050, all six lines, crops at 0.9× and 1×); etexts and BDRC's search for the other witnesses; no model run and no agy turn, the Zhechen Gyaltsab page being typeset and clear.*

Question 435, «ཉེར་ཐམ» in ZG's sums (zg:p300-301): the scan prints «བརྒྱ་དང་ཉེར་ཐམ» for the gold year (img. 344) and the conch year (img. 345, l. 1) and «བརྒྱ་དང་ཉི་ཤུའོ» for the turquoise year; the word is the verse's form of "twenty exactly", so each year is 120 and the three 360. Four witnesses, in [zg-nyer-tham-is-twenty](../../claims/zg-nyer-tham-is-twenty.md): the sum itself; the *Moonbeams*' folio (dz:i050, the same verse with «ཉི་ཤུ» where ZG has «ཉེར་ཐམ»); the word «ཐམ་པ» "exactly" in WB's «དྲུག་ཅུ་ཐམ་པ» (p. 22) and the Life Reckoning's «ཉི་ཤུ་ཐམ་པ», and a grammar's root verses whose «ཉེར་ཐམ» makes 48 + 20 + 31 + 6 = 105; BDRC's search (26 hits, none contradicting). Answered.

Questions 216 and 225, the totals ([meadow-sums-and-the-five-totals](../../claims/meadow-sums-and-the-five-totals.md)): the 360 of WB, the *Moonbeams* and ZG are twelve groups and sixty verdicts; the *Moonbeams* (and a print of it in KD vol. 3, p. 529, whose lines it matches) adds 120 pebbles each for the days and the hours, 600 in all, a view of Khyung lha Gzhon nu bsod nams. The gathering of the verdicts into five totals by the five aspects and their domains, in both the *Moonbeams* and ZG, comes out even (twelve verdicts a total) if each group's five verdicts belong one to each aspect, the first branch of question 216; that is our deduction from the arithmetic, not a sentence of the texts. Question 225 unchanged: both places put the five totals at the close of the meadow's leaves, not of the year of age's household round.

Questions 217 and 259: nothing; no manual of those searched names Sprugtön's limbs, and the whose-element question is not touched by the sums.

Settled as to what it is; why the turtle is called "bone" is not told.

A reading of the notes' "sequence that agrees" through it, not stated by a text: claim [ch27-mewa-triads-zg](../../claims/ch27-mewa-triads-zg.md) beside [mewa-sequence-agreeing](../../claims/mewa-sequence-agreeing.md). Unsettled.

Claim [ch27-element-two-manuals](../../claims/ch27-element-two-manuals.md). Ch. 26 still an inference.

## Read further 2026-10-28 (RD75)

*Read: Steinert's 64 dictionaries (a clone of github.com/christiansteinert/tibetan-dictionary, searched with `locate.py dict` by Wylie headword, whole and by prefix); the etexts of the 1996 print (VEIE0OPI51524892_I1KG12907), Zhechen Gyaltsab's volume (I4PD2444) and, for the search, the *G.ya' sel* (I5876) and the Bhutan collection (I1KG11905); on the scan: WB vol. 2 printed p. 195 (reprint I3CN5029 img. 201, the line with «བརྗེའི་རོ» at 1.6 times) and ZG's img. 348, 349 and 352 (crops at 1.6 and 3.5 times). No model run, no agy turn.*

- Question 365 («བརྗེ»): the dictionaries give «བརྗེ» only as "exchange" (brje, brje ba, brje bo "barter"), never "monk" or "spiritual friend". Zhechen Gyaltsab, listing the same sets in the same order, names the second set's person the spiritual friend (དགེ་བའི་བཤེས) and gives it the same things, the golden vajra, the crystal rosary, the empowerment vase and a maṇḍala (zg:p348-349); so WB's «བརྗེ» set is by that witness the spiritual friend's, and RD51's "the monk's" is narrowed to it ([ch27-brje-set-zg-spiritual-friend](../../claims/ch27-brje-set-zg-spiritual-friend.md)). WB's own funeral list (printed p. 195) sets «བརྗེའི་རོ» between the spiritual friend's corpse and the monk's, so the word is neither there. What «བརྗེ» spells is not shown.
- Question 366: «ན་ཟླ་སྟོན་མོ» is the age-mates' feast (na zla, of the same age; ston mo, a feast). ZG's verse for p. 9, l. 6 reads «ཚང་ཟླ་མག་སྐུད་བུ་ཚར་ངན» on the scan (zg:p352): «ཚང་ཟླ» (comrades, kin of the house), the 1996 print's «ཚད» a misprint; «མག་སྐུད» is the son-in-law's side, and ZG's «སྐུད» agrees with the 1996 print against the Taikhang's «རྒུད». «འུར་ཞོགས» is a headword, "startling talk, an alarm", so p. 17, l. 26 is "an alarm" and not "a raid"; «བུར་ཞོགས» and «ཞུར་ཞོགས» are none. «ལྕོང་མོ» is the tadpole, and its «རྔམས» (splendour) or «རྔ་མས» (tail) of l. 9 is not told by any dictionary. Claim [ch27-words-in-steinert-dictionaries](../../claims/ch27-words-in-steinert-dictionaries.md).
- Question 364: Ives-Waldo has «ཤིང་ལུག་པ» as "a man born in the wood sheep year": པ is the person-suffix, as the scan reads; that the wood sheep's class is the cow stays an inference from the class counts.
- Questions 363 and 368: nothing new. The typeset notes' p. 533 was not looked for again; no dictionary has «ལུས་སྨེ» beyond the *Bod yig tshig gter rgya mtsho* ("the mewa of the year of one's own birth, as skye sme") and «སྨེ་ཕྲེང» (the sixty-year cycle of the mewa, Valby, 84000), which agree with [mewa-sequence-agreeing](../../claims/mewa-sequence-agreeing.md).

## Read further 2026-10-28 (RD77)

*Read: the Taikhang reproduction (I5878) img. 20 (p. 9, l. 6), img. 32 (p. 17, l. 9) and img. 33 (p. 17, l. 26), on the scan by eye at 2 to 5 times in crops of the single line, found by Yigdzin-1's place on the line (Yigdzin-1 run over these three images and the Zhol print's img. 638, 650, 651 for the line offsets only; the Zhol and reprint crops were not repeated). No MITRA, no agy turn.*

- p. 17, l. 26 (img. 33, line 1; the clause comes after «སྐུ་མཁར་ལ་ནི་དམག་གིས་བསྐོར»): «ཞག་བཅུ་ཚུན་ལ» and «ཞོགས་དང» are clear. The syllable between them has its first letter fused with the line's head-stroke and the next letter in one black mass at 5 times, so that neither a བ nor an འ can be told: unsettled by the Taikhang's own ink. Yigdzin-1's «བུར» on the Taikhang therefore carries no weight against the Zhol print's and the 1996 print's «འུར»; the word with a meaning in the dictionaries is «འུར་ཞོགས», "an alarm" ([ch27-words-in-steinert-dictionaries](../../claims/ch27-words-in-steinert-dictionaries.md)), and that is the reading kept, marked as read from the other prints and not from the Taikhang.
- p. 17, l. 9 (img. 32, line 3 as the lines of the whole spread are counted from the top): after «ལྕོང་མོའི» the syllable pair is a ཟ- or ཛ-like head with a stroke after it and «མས», as the 1996 print shows it, before «རྒྱ་མཚོ་དཀྲུགས»; at 4 times the stack of ར over ང that «རྔམས» needs is not told from that ཛ-like shape, and equally not from «རྔ་མས». Unsettled between «རྔམས» (splendour) and «རྔ་མས» (the tail's); the scan allows both.
- p. 9, l. 6 (img. 20, line 2): after «མག» the next syllable is one stack with ུ and a final ད, its lower letter a ག- or ཀ-like shape with a dot inside; the head letter (ར for «རྒུད» or ས for «སྐུད») and the lower letter ག against ཀ are not told at 4 times, so the Taikhang cannot settle «རྒུད» against «སྐུད». RD57's «རྒུད» from the Taikhang by Yigdzin-1 and agy is thus a machine reading; ZG's «སྐུད» on its own scan stands beside the 1996 print's.
- Question 407: that the Taikhang's three lines cannot be told from the Zhol print's by the eye is itself the answer on these lines: no difference between the impressions is shown by the scan; the differences are Yigdzin-1's. The pair «ཆུ་མིག» against «ཆུ་མག» (the water source, p. 9, l. 6) was not looked at again.

## Open

- Question 363 ([q0363](../../questions/q0363.md)): The la of one born in a snake year and of one born in a horse year both wander "to the north" as printed (WB vol. 2, p. 16, l. 24, on the reprint I3CN5029 img. 22 too), and those of the rat and pig years both to the south: is one of each pair a misprint for east or west, and which?
- Question 364 ([q0364](../../questions/q0364.md)): In the golden turtle (WB vol. 2, p. 20, l. 14), is the wood sheep's class «ཤིང་ལུག་པ» the cow (བ, ba), as the identifying pebbles and "the cow and the turtle agree with all" suggest, and by which element does a year "become" wood, fire, iron, water or earth in this reckoning?
- Question 365 ([q0365](../../questions/q0365.md)): For whom are the four reckonings of «བརྗེ» counted from the five rats (WB vol. 2, p. 23, l. 22): monks (བཙུན, btsun, or a form of «བྲེ»), as their vajra, discipline and begging suggest?
- Question 366 ([q0366](../../questions/q0366.md)): What are «ཚད་ཟླ་མག་སྐུད» (p. 9, l. 6), «ན་ཟླ་སྟོན་མོ» (p. 9, l. 16), the raid «ཞུར[?]/འུར་ཞོགས» (p. 17, l. 26) and «ལྕོང་མོའི་ཛ[?]་མས» (p. 17, l. 9)? The scan leaves the third and fourth letters in doubt; the others are clear but not understood.
- Question 367 ([q0367](../../questions/q0367.md)): In the tenth circle's fine way (WB vol. 2, p. 10, l. 25), what is «རུས་ནག», "the black bone", on which the pig, sheep and hare are counted, as against the turquoise dragon and the red bird of the other nails?
- Question 368 ([q0368](../../questions/q0368.md)): In the circles' mewa pebble, how are the two mewas the notes name (the reckoned person's body mewa, and the starting point's mewa that agrees with it in the mewa sequence; vol. 2, p. 533) combined into the one counted up?
- Question 369 ([q0369](../../questions/q0369.md)): The first circle's luck remedy, «[…]ང་གསོ་འཛུགས» (p. 4, l. 7): the first syllable is broken on both the 1996 scan and the reprint; does another print (WBZ, the Taikhang) read it?
- Question 407 ([q0407](../../questions/q0407.md)): The Taikhang reproduction and the Zhol print read the same on most lines of ch. 27 but not on p. 9 l. 6 («མག་རྒུད» against «མག་སྐུད»), p. 17 l. 26 («བུར» against «འུར») and the same line's water source («ཆུ་མིག» against «ཆུ་མག»), by Yigdzin-1: are they two impressions of different blocks, or one block read differently by the machine, and which of each pair does a reader of the print see?
- Question 391 ([q0391](../../questions/q0391.md)): What is the meaning of «རྟ་ཞག་སྔོན་པོ» (vol. 1, p. 460) and of «ཐེ་ཁྱིམ་བཙུན་མོ» (p. 462)?
- Question 435 ([q0435](../../questions/q0435.md)): In Zhechen Gyaltsab's sums of the meadow's ornament (zg:p300-301, scan img. 344-345), what is «ཉེར་ཐམ» in «བརྒྱ་དང་ཉེར་ཐམ» ("a hundred and nyer tham") for the gold and the conch years, against «བརྒྱ་དང་ཉི་ཤུ» for the turquoise: is it twenty, so that each year comes to 120 and the three to 360?

## Searched and not found

- **A note on ch. 27's small reckonings in Mipham's section eight** (RD38, 2026-10-23; vol. 2, pp. 535–538 read whole on the reprint, I3CN5029 img. 541–544): none; the section glosses ch. 30's reckoning of the moment and ch. 28's omens and closes the notes. Its only words on the circles are section seven's (p. 533).
